    - [Now Assist in Document Management](now-assist-document-management.md) -- Use ServiceNow Now Assist in Document Management to get key insights from the document through interactive Q&A, and auto- generated common questions. You can enable your agents to understand the documents so that they can give quick insights.
      - [Explore](explore-now-assist-in-document-management.md) -- Now Assist in Document Management uses generative AI to extract key insights from documents through seamless conversational interactions, reducing manual effort while improving accuracy and consistency.
      - [Configure](configure-now-assist-document-management.md) -- If you have the admin role, you can configure Now Assist in Document Management in the application enabling the use of its generative AI skills within the application workspace.
        - [Configure the Smart Documents Skill for Now Assist in Document Management](configure-skill-smart-documents.md) -- Configure the Smart Document skill to get document insights through seamless conversational interactions by using Now Assist in Document Management application.
          - [System property for Smart Documents skill](system-property-smart-documents.md) -- Configure and set the system property to use Smart Documents skill.
      - [Use](use-now-assist-document-management.md) -- If you have a sn_nowassist_admin.nsa_admin role, you can use Now Assist in Document Management to quickly summarize documents and get insights.
        - [Generate a document summary](generate-document-summary-now-assist.md) -- Generate a document summary to get quick insights into key information from documents by using Now Assist in Document Management.
        - [Generate summary and ask questions](ask-questions-faqs-now-assist.md) -- Generate summary and ask questions to gain quick insights from your documents by using Now Assist in Document Management.
    - [Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/knowledge-management.md) -- The ServiceNow Knowledge Management (KM) application enables the sharing of information in knowledge bases. These knowledge bases contain articles that provide users with information such as self-help, troubleshooting, and task resolution.
      - [Exploring Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/exploring-knowledge-management.md) -- Learn more about Knowledge Management with a sample workflow and review the benefits it can provide for different users in your organization.
        - [Sitemap Generator for the Knowledge Portal](knowledge-management/sitemap-generator-km.md) -- The ServiceNow Sitemap Generator application enables you to efficiently define and automatically generate XML sitemaps.
        - [I18N - Knowledge internationalization](knowledge-management/c_I18NKMInternational.md) -- The optional knowledge internationalization features enable knowledge users to view articles in multiple languages.
        - [Scoped knowledge bases](knowledge-management/scoped-knowledge-base-administration.md) -- To protect knowledge bases containing sensitive articles, use a scoped knowledge base. Even system administrators and knowledge administrators can't administer scoped knowledge bases unless explicitly authorized through user criteria.
        - [Machine learning solutions for Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/machine-learning-km.md) -- Machine learning in Knowledge Management helps improve knowledge governance and quality of your knowledge base.
          - [Predictive Intelligence for Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/predictive-intelligence-for-km.md) -- The Predictive Intelligence for Knowledge Management capability uses machine-learning algorithms to provide various Knowledge Management capabilities, such as similar articles and knowledge demand insights.
        - [Knowledge article templates](knowledge-management/knowledge-article-templates.md) -- Article templates have pre-defined fields structured in a specific order. These templates help create a consistent structure for knowledge articles.
        - [Knowledge blocks](knowledge-management/knowledge-blocks.md) -- Knowledge blocks are reusable pieces of content secured by customizable user criteria that you can add to knowledge articles in a knowledge base. The user criteria determine which user roles can or can’t read the block content in an article, enabling only relevant content to be visible to that user.
        - [Knowledge subscriptions](knowledge-management/knowledge-article-subscriptions.md) -- Subscribe to knowledge bases and knowledge articles to receive email notifications about new articles and article revisions or comments.
        - [Knowledge demand insights](knowledge-management/knowledge-demand-insights.md) -- Identify knowledge gaps and improve content of your knowledge base using the Knowledge Demand Insights feature.
        - [Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/self-service-analytics.md) -- The Self-Service Analytics framework enables you to gather information about user activities and deflection outcomes related to those activities.
          - [Self-Service Analytics concepts](knowledge-management/ssa-concepts.md) -- You can quantify your self-service effectiveness by measuring case deflections.
        - [Translation management](knowledge-management/translation-management.md) -- Knowledge articles are authored and published in many languages and translating these articles to languages other than the language they are authored in requires creating translation tasks. Translation management enables you to manage these translation tasks.
        - [Knowledge-Centered Service configuration](knowledge-management/knowledge-centred-configuration.md) -- Knowledge-Centered Service (KCS) is a method for the creation and continuous improvement of knowledge based on the experience of agents and the patterns observed by knowledge reuse.
        - [Article quality index for knowledge management](knowledge-management/knowledge-article-quality-index.md) -- Assess the quality of knowledge articles with the article quality index (AQI). The AQI helps maintain consistent quality of knowledge articles attached to a knowledge base where articles are written by various authors.
        - [Article versioning](knowledge-management/article-versioning.md) -- Use the Knowledge Management article versioning feature to create and maintain multiple versions of a knowledge article.
        - [Ownership groups](knowledge-management/enable-ownership-group.md) -- Configure ownership groups and associate them with knowledge articles to maintain article quality, manage approvals, and for timely resolution of feedback.
        - [Automating feedback management to improve content](knowledge-management/actionable-knowledge-feedback.md) -- Manage high volumes of article feedback by enabling task generation for negative feedback. When an article is rated poorly or marked as unhelpful, a feedback task is generated.
        - [Migrate to Knowledge Management v3](knowledge-management/c_KMv3Migration.md) -- When you migrate to Knowledge Management v3 from v2, you must migrate legacy knowledge content and any customizations you have made to the Knowledge Base applications.
        - [Migration from workflow to flow](knowledge-management/migration-from-workflow-to-flow.md) -- Migration from Workflow to Flow in Knowledge Management includes migration of publishing and retiring workflows to the flow designer framework.
        - [Enhancements to knowledge article view page](knowledge-management/enhancements-to-knowledge-article-view-page.md) -- Enhancement to the UI components on the article view page improves their visibility and utility. The mobile responsive UI has been built to enhance the visual experience for those using Knowledge Management on a mobile device.
      - [Configuring Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/configuring-knowledge-management.md) -- Plan and configure your Knowledge Management implementation.
        - [Activate the Knowledge Management Advanced plugin](knowledge-management/activate-knowledge-advanced-plugin.md) -- Activate the Knowledge Management Advanced plugin (com.snc.knowledge_advanced) to enable advanced features for Knowledge Management.
        - [Knowledge Management setup guide for admins](knowledge-management/setup-knowledge-admin.md) -- Before users in your organization can start creating knowledge bases and knowledge articles, you must set up Knowledge Management. Work with stakeholders to define requirements for setting up Knowledge Management effectively to meet the needs of users.
        - [Knowledge base setup guide for knowledge admins and managers](knowledge-management/set-up-knowledge-admin-user.md) -- After basic Knowledge Management setup is completed, you can set up a knowledge base for users to create and publish knowledge articles.
        - [Activate the sitemap configuration and definition records for the Knowledge Portal](knowledge-management/config-sitemap-generator.md) -- Include knowledge articles in your sitemap by activating the sitemap configuration and definition records.
        - [Activate the knowledge management internationalization plugin](knowledge-management/c_ActKMIntPlugin.md) -- To enable translation of knowledge articles, you must activate multiple plugins.
        - [Activate knowledge blocks](knowledge-management/activate-knowledge-blocks.md) -- You can activate knowledge blocks [com.snc.knowledge_blocks] if you have the admin role. This plugin includes demo data and activates related plugins if they are not already active.
        - [Enable knowledge blocks for each knowledge base](knowledge-management/enable-knowledge-blocks-for-knowledge-base.md) -- Enable the knowledge blocks feature for each knowledge base where you plan to use the blocks. Once enabled, you can create knowledge blocks to add to knowledge articles within a knowledge base.
        - [Configure translation management](knowledge-management/configure-translation-management.md) -- Configure translation management to create translation tasks.
          - [Activate translation management](knowledge-management/activate-translation-mgt.md) -- You can activate the Knowledge Management Advanced (com.snc.knowledge_advanced) and I18N:Knowledge Management Internationalization Plugin v2 (com.glideapp.knowledge.i18n2) plugins for translation management if you have the admin role.
          - [Add a custom Localization Framework setting to enable bulk translations](knowledge-management/conf-lf-settings-manual-translations.md) -- Add a custom Localization Framework setting to translate multiple knowledge articles manually.
          - [Add a custom Localization Framework setting to enable machine translation](knowledge-management/con-lf-dynamic-translations.md) -- Add a custom Localization Framework setting to enable machine translation for bulk translations of knowledge articles.
        - [Activate Knowledge Management KCS Capabilities](knowledge-management/activate-kcs-capabilties-plugin.md) -- You can activate the Knowledge Management KCS Capabilities plugin (com.snc.knowledge_kcs_capabilities) to enable the use of certain KCS roles and KCS article states.
        - [Enable languages for a knowledge base](knowledge-management/enable-language-for-knowledge-base.md) -- Select the languages you want enabled at a knowledge base level. Users can then create articles only for selected languages.
        - [Configure the Knowledge Management Service Portal](knowledge-management/knowledge-management-service-portal.md) -- The Knowledge Management Service Portal enables users to perform the same operations that are available with the Knowledge Management V3 plugin. From the portal, users can view knowledge bases and articles, search for information, sort and filter search results, and provide feedback.
          - [Enable external or public users to view knowledge articles from the Knowledge Management Service Portal](knowledge-management/make-knowledge-visible-to-public.md) -- Enable knowledge articles on the Knowledge Management Service Portal to be visible to external or public users.
          - [Knowledge Management Service Portal widgets](knowledge-management/km-service-portal-widget-instances.md) -- The Knowledge Management Service Portal uses a number of widgets to enable searches, display search results, and display articles, related articles, and related items.
            - [Activate the Knowledge Management Service Portal plugin](knowledge-management/activate-km-service-portal-plugin.md) -- The Knowledge Management - Service Portal plugin (com.snc.knowledge_serviceportal) is active by default for customers on Madrid and later releases. Existing customers on release versions prior to Madrid can activate the plugin, if required.
            - [Configure search widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-search-widget-options.md) -- Configure widget instance options for the search widgets on the Knowledge Management Service Portal homepage and search results page.
            - [Configure knowledge base tile widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-kb-tile-widget-options.md) -- Configure widget instance options for the knowledge base tile widgets on the Knowledge Management Service Portal homepage.
            - [Configure the home page widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-homepage-widget-options.md) -- Configure widget instance options for the widgets used to display popular articles on the Knowledge Management Service Portal homepage.
            - [Configure sort widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-search-results-sort-options.md) -- Configure widget instance options for the knowledge sort widget on the Knowledge Management Service Portal search results page.
            - [Configure article list widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-article-list-widget-options.md) -- Configure widget instance options for the article lists on the Knowledge Management Service Portal homepage.
            - [Configure search results list widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-search-results-list-options.md) -- Configure widget instance options for the results list widget on the Knowledge Management Service Portal search results page.
            - [Configure filter facet widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-search-results-filter-facets.md) -- Configure widget instance options for the filter facet widgets on the Knowledge Management Service Portal search results page.
            - [Configure a user action for the article view page](knowledge-management/config-user-action-on-article-view.md) -- Configure a user action for the Knowledge Management article view page in the Knowledge Management Service Portal.
            - [Configure article helpful widget instance options](knowledge-management/config-knowledge-article-helpful.md) -- Configure the Knowledge Article Helpful widget instance options for the Knowledge Management article view page in the Knowledge Management Service Portal.
            - [Configure article comments instance options](knowledge-management/config-knowledge-article-comments.md) -- Configure the Knowledge Article Comments widget instance options for the Knowledge Management article view page in the Knowledge Management Service Portal.
            - [Configure related catalog item widget instance options](knowledge-management/configure-related-items-widget.md) -- Configure the Related Catalog Item widget instance options for the Knowledge Management article view page in the Knowledge Management Service Portal. These options relate to the list of catalog items mapped to an article.
            - [Configure knowledge related articles widget instance options](knowledge-management/configure-related-articles-widget.md) -- Configure the Knowledge Related Articles widget instance options for the Knowledge Management article view page in the Knowledge Management Service Portal. These options relate to the list of related articles mapped to an article.
          - [Search using the Knowledge Management Service Portal](knowledge-management/knowledge-service-portal-searching.md) -- Enter one or more words in the search bar on the Knowledge Management Service Portal homepage to view all search results.
          - [Integrating Knowledge Management features with the Service Portal](knowledge-management/conf-service-portal-know-management.md) -- To create a consistent user experience for Knowledge Management, integrate Knowledge Management Service Portal in your end-user facing Service Portal.
            - [Integrate the Knowledge Management homepage with the Service Portal homepage](knowledge-management/conf-service-portal-know-management.md) -- To create a consistent user experience for Knowledge Management, integrate Knowledge Management Service Portal in your end-user facing Service Portal.
            - [Integrate the Knowledge Management search results page with the Service Portal search results page](knowledge-management/conf-service-portal-know-management.md) -- To create a consistent user experience for Knowledge Management, integrate Knowledge Management Service Portal in your end-user facing Service Portal.
            - [Add widgets to access knowledge articles from the Service Portal homepage](knowledge-management/conf-service-portal-know-management.md) -- To create a consistent user experience for Knowledge Management, integrate Knowledge Management Service Portal in your end-user facing Service Portal.
          - [Configure custom SEO tags for canonical URLs and localized knowledge articles](knowledge-management/configure-seo-knowledge.md) -- Discover relevant knowledge articles through search engines by adding custom SEO tags for canonical URLs and hreflang tags to the knowledge article view page.
          - [Enable and configure AI Search for Knowledge Management Service Portal](knowledge-management/enable-and-configure-ai-search-for-km-service-portal.md) -- Enable and define the AI Search experience in Knowledge Management Service Portal.
        - [Create a knowledge base](knowledge-management/create-a-knowledgebase.md) -- Create a knowledge base to provide a self-service platform for users to store, share, and manage content. Configure knowledge bases into product or service categories and manage user access based on permissions. Customize workflows for publishing and retiring articles in the knowledge base.
        - [Configure knowledge bases for a portal](knowledge-management/enable-search-on-all-kb.md) -- Select which knowledge bases are displayed in a portal to view the associated knowledge articles. If no knowledge bases are configured, users can view articles from all knowledge bases in the portal.
        - [Configure knowledge subscriptions](knowledge-management/configure-subscription-properties.md) -- Configure knowledge subscriptions by activating the Knowledge Management Advanced plugin and setting the subscription properties.

          - [Define a knowledge article category](knowledge-management/t_DefineAKnowledgeCategory.md) -- Each knowledge base has a hierarchy of categories that organizes the articles.
          - [Assign a knowledge base manager](knowledge-management/t_AssignAKnowledgeBaseManager.md) -- You can assign users as managers of a knowledge base.
        - [Configure Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/config-ssa.md) -- Configure the Self-Service Analytics framework so you can use the generated metrics in a dashboard.
          - [Activate Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/activate-ssa-cs.md) -- Activate the Self-Service Analytics Core plugin (com.snc.self_service_analytics_core) if you have the admin role. This plugin activates related plugins if they are not already active.
          - [Configure activity contexts for Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/configure-activity-context.md) -- Define the type of activities you want to collect as Self-Service Analytics data for a user entity such as consumers and customer contacts.
          - [Configure pattern elements for Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/configure-pattern-element.md) -- Configure a pattern element to specify a single activity type and how many times it occurs. Each pattern element is implemented as regular expressions.
          - [Configure pattern element groups for Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/configure-pattern-element-group.md) -- Define a logical combination of two pattern elements, pattern element group, or both and how many times the combination occurs. Each pattern element group is implemented as regular expressions.
          - [Configure activity patterns for Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/configure-activity-pattern.md) -- Configure an activity pattern to combine the pattern element, pattern element group, or both for a user entity and link them to an outcome.
          - [Set up the deflection configuration for Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/configure-deflection-ssa.md) -- Associate a period of time with an activity context that defines how long a system will wait for a case to be created to match a deflection pattern.
          - [Configure scheduled jobs for Self-Service Analytics](knowledge-management/configure-scheduled-job-ssa.md) -- Schedule when to run the deflection configuration to generate the activities that match an activity pattern within an activity window.
        - [Managing access to knowledge bases and knowledge articles](knowledge-management/user-access-knowledge.md) -- Determine whether certain users or categories of users can access knowledge bases and knowledge articles by controlling contribute and read access.

          - [Control access at the knowledge base level through user criteria](knowledge-management/t_SelectUserCriteria.md) -- You specify user criteria for a knowledge base to control which users are granted access to read and contribute knowledge articles to that knowledge base.
          - [Control access at the knowledge article level through user criteria](knowledge-management/t_SelectUCArticle.md) -- You specify user criteria for a knowledge article to control which users are granted read access to the knowledge article.

This plugin activates related plugins if they are not already installed.
          - [Deploy Knowledge Management - Add-in for Microsoft Word](knowledge-management/deploy-km-word-manifest.md) -- Deploy the Knowledge Management - Add-in for Microsoft Word for authoring knowledge articles from within the Microsoft Word.

          - [Enable downloading of the source Microsoft Word document for a knowledge article](knowledge-management/enable-downloading-article-word-document.md) -- Set the sn_km_word.glide.knowman.enable_document_download property to enable users to download the source Microsoft Word document of a knowledge article from the knowledge article view page in a portal or workspace.
        - [User criteria diagnostics for Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/diagnose-knowledge-user-criteria.md) -- Manage user access to a knowledge base or an article using user criteria diagnostics. Understand what access users have to a knowledge base or an article and determine which user criteria define those permissions.
          - [Diagnose user access to a knowledge base](knowledge-management/diagnose-access-criteria-at-kb-level.md) -- Identify which users have access to a knowledge base and what enables that access.
          - [Diagnose user access to an article](knowledge-management/diagnose-access-criteria-at-an-article-level.md) -- Identify which users have access to an article and what enables that access.
          - [Configure access to knowledge bases for unauthenticated users](knowledge-management/configure-unauthenticated-user.md) -- Identify knowledge bases accessible to unauthenticated users based on the user criteria and the system property settings.
          - [Collect knowledge usage metrics](knowledge-management/Guest-user-diagnostic.md) -- Capture metrics such as article views, page views, and portal searches to track aggregated knowledge usage by users.

          - [Restrict access to fields in an article template](knowledge-management/secure-field-article-template.md) -- Use encryption modules to set field-level security in article templates and display the fields based on role permissions.
          - [Generate SEO information for articles using article templates](knowledge-management/configure-SEO-article-template.md) -- Improve the searchability of knowledge articles by using the content in an article template field to generate SEO tags for articles created using that template. Search Engine Optimization (SEO) optimise searches in public search engine.
        - [Integration with external knowledge sources](knowledge-management/knowledge-external-content-integration.md) -- Use the external content integration feature to integrate content from various external sources and enable unified knowledge search results.
          - [Activate the External Content Integration plugin](knowledge-management/act-ext-know-content.md) -- The External Content Integration feature is activated with the Knowledge Management - External Content Integration plugin (com.snc.knowledge.external_integration).

Define import parameters for the external knowledge source.
          - [Import content from an external knowledge source](knowledge-management/run-import-job-external-content-integration.md) -- Run an import job manually or set up a schedule to process the integrated external content.

To open knowledge search results in a host system, configure the URL to the external source system to open articles from search results directly in the source system.

            - [Integrate external knowledge sources into the Knowledge Management application](knowledge-management/sample-ext-cont-integration.md) -- You can integrate content from multiple external sources so your users can acquire and search knowledge from a single location. Use this sample configuration to create a connection to your external account, configure external knowledge sources, and import content to enable search results for unified content.

        - [Configuration tasks for knowledge demand insights](knowledge-management/configuration-tasks-for-knowledge-demand-insights.md) -- Review the following configurations tasks for knowledge demand insights.

          - [Configure solution definitions to find gaps in a knowledge base](knowledge-management/configure-km-solution-defintions.md) -- The Knowledge Demand Insights feature finds knowledge gaps by comparing knowledge bases with existing tasks. To enable comparing knowledge bases with a task type, you must first configure similarity and clustering solution definitions.
          - [Configure the mapping of solution definitions with task tables](knowledge-management/select-tasks-knowledge-bases.md) -- Configure the combination of the task table and similarity and clustering solution definitions that are processed in a scheduled job for a task type.         - [Using Now Mobile for Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/mobile-experience-for-km.md) -- Access knowledge articles from anywhere using the Information applet on the Now Mobile app. You can view recently viewed and most popular articles, search for articles, browse articles by category, and provide feedback for articles.
          - [View the most popular or recently viewed articles in the Now Mobile Information applet](knowledge-management/access-articles-now-mobile.md) -- The Information applet on the Now Mobile app enables you to access knowledge articles from a mobile device.

          - [Search for an article in the Now Mobile Information applet](knowledge-management/search-article-now-mobile.md) -- You can search for an article in the Now Mobile Information applet.
          - [View an article in the Now Mobile Information applet](knowledge-management/view-article-now-mobile.md) -- You can view the author, published date, number of views, and the overall rating details of the article in the Now Mobile Information applet. In addition, you can respond to and evaluate articles and view related items and related articles.
          - [Provide feedback for a knowledge article in the Now Mobile Information applet](knowledge-management/respond-evaluate-articles.md) -- You can contribute to feedback on knowledge articles by rating an article, marking an article as helpful or not helpful, and posting and replying to comments for an article in the Now Mobile Information applet.

        - [Using knowledge blocks](knowledge-management/using-knowlege-blocks.md) -- Use knowledge blocks to simplify knowledge authoring for writers and knowledge consumption for readers.
          - [Create a knowledge block](knowledge-management/create-modify-knowledge-block.md) -- Create or modify a knowledge block to define a reusable piece of content that can be inserted into knowledge articles in a knowledge base. The knowledge block is secured by user criteria, which controls what users, groups, roles, companies, locations, or departments can read or not read the content in an article or search, enabling users to more easily view content that is relevant to them.
            - [Select user criteria for a knowledge block](knowledge-management/select-user-criteria-for-knowledge-block.md) -- Control which users can read or not read knowledge block content within an article in a knowledge base by setting user criteria at the knowledge base and knowledge block level. As a knowledge contributor, you can apply user criteria at the knowledge block level.
            - [Retire a knowledge block](knowledge-management/retire-knowledge-block.md) -- You can retire a published knowledge block so that it's no longer available for users to view or search.
          - [Add knowledge blocks to a knowledge article](knowledge-management/add-knowledge-block-to-knowledge-article.md) -- You can insert one or more knowledge blocks into a knowledge article within a knowledge base. Each knowledge block is secured by user criteria, which controls who can read or not read the content in an article.
          - [Preview a knowledge article with knowledge blocks](knowledge-management/preview-knowledge-article-with-knowledge-blocks.md) -- Preview a knowledge article with knowledge blocks by user or, if published, by date. For an unpublished article, you can preview the article by user by impersonating different users to see how the article displays or hides block content (based on the user criteria set at the knowledge base and knowledge block level). For published articles, you can preview the article by both user and date to see how the article appears in past versions for different users.

            - [Edit a knowledge block in Agent Workspace](knowledge-management/edit-knowledge-block-workspace.md) -- Edit knowledge blocks within a knowledge base in Agent Workspace to update the reused content across articles within the knowledge base.
            - [Publish a knowledge block in Agent Workspace](knowledge-management/publish-block-agent.md) -- Publish a knowledge block to make it available to add to knowledge articles.
            - [Approve a knowledge block in Agent Workspace](knowledge-management/approve-knowledge-block-agent.md) -- Approve a knowledge block that is awaiting your review in Agent Workspace.
            - [Add a knowledge block to a knowledge article in Agent Workspace](knowledge-management/add-knowledge-block-to-article-workspace.md) -- Add a knowledge block to a knowledge article in Agent Workspace.
            - [Preview a knowledge article with knowledge blocks in Agent Workspace](knowledge-management/preview_blocks_agent.md) -- Preview a knowledge article with knowledge blocks in Agent Workspace.
            - [Retire a knowledge block in Agent Workspace](knowledge-management/retire-block-agent.md) -- Retire a published knowledge block if you no longer want it to be available for users to view or search.

            - [KBPortalServiceImpl - Global](knowledge-management/KBPortalServiceImplAPI.md) -- The KBPortalServiceImpl API is included with Knowledge Management V3 [com.snc.knowledge3] as a script include. It provides methods to use with knowledge, such as integration with a custom search.

              - [KBBlock - KBBlock()](knowledge-management/KBBlockAPI.md) -- The KBBlock API is included with knowledge blocks [com.snc.knowledge_blocks] as a script include. It provides methods to use with the knowledge blocks feature, such as integration with a custom knowledge article viewer.

Identify the language experts and create assignment rules so that translation tasks are automatically assigned to language experts and groups.

          - [Assign an AQI checklist to a knowledge base](knowledge-management/assign-an-aqi-checklist-to-a-knowledgebase.md) -- Before a knowledge reviewer can start performing the AQI review, you must assign an AQI checklist to a knowledge base.

          - [Delete questions from an AQI checklist](knowledge-management/delete-questions-from-an-aqi-checklist.md) -- Deleting a question in a checklist deletes the text in the question of previously performed AQI reviews that use the checklist.         - [Domain separation and Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/domain-separation-knowledge.md) -- Domain separation is supported in Knowledge Management. Domain separation enables you to separate data, processes, and administrative tasks into logical groupings called domains. You can control several aspects of this separation, including which users can see and access data.

It also introduces new fields and related lists to the Knowledge form, new columns to the Knowledge list, and updates to Knowledge dashboard reports.

        - [Validity of a knowledge article](knowledge-management/article-validity.md) -- Knowledge article validity is the date a knowledge article expires. Articles do not appear in search results after the Valid to date or if the Valid to date is blank.

        - [Knowledge workflows](knowledge-management/r_KnowledgeWorkflows.md) -- The publishing and retirement processes for a knowledge article are controlled by workflows defined for the knowledge base that the article belongs to.
      - [Analytics and Reporting Solutions for Knowledge Management](knowledge-management/analytics-and-reporting-solutions-for-knowlege-management.md) -- The following topics provide information on Platform Analytics Solutions and Demand Insights dashboards for Knowledge Management.
        - [Knowledge Management Platform Analytics Solutions](knowledge-management/knowledge-content-pack.md) -- Platform Analytics Solutions contain preconfigured dashboards. These dashboards contain actionable data visualizations that help you improve your business processes and practices.
          - [Knowledge Management dashboard](knowledge-management/knowledge-management-dashboard.md) -- The Knowledge Management dashboard helps knowledge content owners and managers to determine the usage and quality of their knowledge content. It helps confirm that the proper processes and procedures are being followed for content creation.
          - [Knowledge Management Overview dashboard](knowledge-management/km-overview-dash.md) -- The Knowledge Management Overview dashboard provides an overview of various usage metrics associated with knowledge articles. You can view and track knowledge usage and views and direct the efforts of content owners more efficiently.
            - [Update the navigation path for the Knowledge Management Overview dashboard](knowledge-management/km-overview-dash-path.md) -- Update the navigation path to access the Knowledge Management Overview dashboard.
        - [Pareto chart for Knowledge Demand Insights](knowledge-management/pareto-report-demand-insights.md) -- The Pareto chart for Knowledge Demand Insights enables you to visualize candidate knowledge gaps and prioritize the order in which knowledge gaps should be resolved.
        - [Demand Insights for Cases dashboard](knowledge-management/demand-insights-cases-dashboard.md) -- The Demand Insights for Cases dashboard enables you as a customer service case manager to identify which cases have no or insufficient knowledge coverage. You can then create feedback tasks for knowledge gaps to deflect such cases.
        - [Demand Insights for Incidents dashboard](knowledge-management/demand-insights-incidents-dashboard.md) -- The Demand Insights for Incidents dashboard enables you as an incident manager to identify which incidents have no or insufficient knowledge coverage. You can then create feedback tasks for knowledge gaps to deflect such incidents.
        - [Demand Insights for HR Cases dashboard](knowledge-management/demand-insights-hrcases-dashboard.md) -- The Demand Insights for HR cases dashboard enables you as an HR agent to identify which HR cases have no or insufficient knowledge coverage. You can then create feedback tasks for knowledge gaps to deflect such HR cases.
    - [Knowledge Center](knowledge-center.md) -- The ServiceNow Knowledge Center (KC) is designed to simplify the discovery and oversight of the organizational knowledge.
      - [Exploring Knowledge Center](exploring-knowledge-center.md) -- The Knowledge Center Knowledge Center helps in the effective management of knowledge articles by using an intuitive interface. Beyond traditional knowledge bases, the Knowledge Center provides actionable insights and identifies gaps so that your content stays relevant and accurate.
        - [Knowledge Center home page](kc-home-page.md) -- Knowledge Center is a plugin within Knowledge Management that helps in the effective management of knowledge articles by using an intuitive interface.
        - [Knowledge Center article editor](kc-article-editor.md) -- The Knowledge Center article editor has editing tools that help in formatting knowledge article content such as text, images, and media.
        - [Knowledge Center Article Optimization](knowledge-center-article-optimization.md) -- Article Optimization is an automated system designed to improve the quality and health of knowledge articles, providing actionable feedback to authors and managers.
        - [Potential knowledge gaps](understanding-knowledge-gaps.md) -- Potential knowledge gaps represent areas where you frequently encounter issues but lack supporting articles. Managing these gaps promotes better coverage and improved user experience.
        - [Knowledge blocks in Knowledge Center](kc-knowledge-blocks.md) -- Knowledge blocks are reusable pieces of content secured by customizable user criteria that you can add to knowledge articles in a knowledge base. The user criteria determine which user roles can or can’t read the block content in an article, enabling only relevant content to be visible to that user.
      - [Configuring Knowledge Center](configuring-knowledge-center.md) -- Configure the knowledge content recommendation skill to enable agents to utilize the context menu to elaborate or shorten a knowledge article in the TinyMCE. With the new enhance content editor available with Knowledge Center, agents can create and update the knowledge article with custom instructions.
        - [Enable system properties for Knowledge Center](enable-system-properties-for-KC.md) -- To use the Knowledge Center plugin, you must enable the system properties before you configure the space.
        - [Configuring Article Optimization jobs](configuring-article-optimization-jobs.md) -- Article Optimization jobs are used to define a schedule to run scans along with their conditional settings, if any. Each job will have one or more scans associated with it.
        - [Configure custom script based scans for Article Optimization](configure-custom-AO-scan.md) -- The Out-of-Box (OOB) Knowledge Center settings provide default configurations for article optimization scans. However, users can configure new scans for article optimization.
        - [Enable knowledge blocks in the Knowledge Center](kc-enable-knowledge-blocks.md) -- Enable the knowledge blocks feature for each knowledge base where you plan to use the blocks. Once enabled, you can create knowledge blocks to add to knowledge articles within a knowledge base.
      - [Using Knowledge Center](using-knowledge-center.md) -- Learn how to manage and enhance your knowledge base using the powerful tools and workflows available in Knowledge Center.
        - [Search knowledge article](search-knowledge-article.md) -- Use the search function to find articles you want to work on and then filter and sort your results as needed.
        - [Check Knowledge Center insights](check-gaps-duplicates-optimization.md) -- Learn how to access detailed views for gaps, duplicates, or article optimization from the Insights section in the Knowledge Center.
        - [Manage potential knowledge gaps](address-knowledge-gaps.md) -- Learn how to review and resolve potential knowledge gaps in the Knowledge Center.
        - [Identify and resolve duplicate articles](identify-duplicate-articles.md) -- Learn how to find and manage duplicate knowledge articles in the Knowledge Center.
        - [Address items that requires attention](using-requires-attention.md) -- Learn how to review and manage items that need your attention, such as expiring articles, flagged content, and feedback tasks.
        - [Generate and edit articles with the article editor](kc-edit-knowledge-article.md) -- The Knowledge Center (KC) Article Editor delivers an intuitive experience for creating and managing content. Featuring blocks, responsive previews, and AI-powered assistance, it simplifies article design while verifying accessibility and search engine optimization compliance. Backward compatibility with existing templates guarantees a smooth transition for authors and administrators.
        - [View article optimization analysis](view-article-optimization.md) -- Learn how to navigate to Article optimization in Knowledge Center.
        - [Article optimization with article length scan](article-optimization-with-article-length-scan.md) -- The article length scan is a script‑based, non‑AI scan that runs in the background while authors work on articles. When the scan detects a length issue, an article length card appears in the Article Optimization panel.
        - [Review and optimize articles with Article Optimization](kc-review-and-optimize-articles.md) -- The Knowledge Center Article Optimization feature provides automated, actionable feedback to improve content quality, accessibility, and search engine optimization, with configurable scans to identify and fix issues.
        - [Using knowledge blocks in the Knowledge Center](using-knowlege-blocks-in-kc.md) -- Use knowledge blocks to simplify knowledge authoring for writers and knowledge consumption for readers.
          - [Edit a knowledge block in a knowledge article](kc-edit-knowledge-block-in-a-knowledge-article.md) -- Edit knowledge blocks within a knowledge article to update the reused content across articles within the knowledge base.
          - [Add a knowledge block to a knowledge article](kc-add-knowledge-blocks-to-a-knowledge-article.md) -- Insert one or more knowledge blocks into a knowledge article within a knowledge base. Each knowledge block is secured by user criteria, which controls who can read or not read the content in an article.
          - [Create a knowledge block in the Knowledge Center](kc-create-a-knowledge-block.md) -- Create a knowledge block to define a reusable piece of content that can be inserted into knowledge articles in a knowledge base. The knowledge block is secured by user criteria, which controls what users, groups, and roles, can read the content in an article, allowing display of content that is relevant only to them.
          - [Knowledge Health score](knowledge-health-score.md) -- Knowledge health score is a calculate the score.
    - [Now Assist in Knowledge Management](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/now-assist-knowledge-management.md) -- Use ServiceNow Now Assist in Knowledge Management to generate a new knowledge article for a case or incident via the Now Assist panel, classic environment, or the respective configurable workspace. Agents can use these articles to propose quicker resolutions to customers.
      - [Explore Now Assist in Knowledge Management](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/exploring-now-assist-KM.md) -- Use the Now Assist in Knowledge Management application to help agents generate a new knowledge article for a case or incident via the Now Assist panel, classic environment, and configurable workspace.
      - [Configure Now Assist in Knowledge Management](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/configuring-now-assist-km.md) -- If you have the admin role, you can configure the Now Assist in the Knowledge Management application, enabling agents to use its generative AI skills within their application workspace and the classic environment.
        - [Configure the KB generation skill](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-configuring-km-skills.md) -- Configure the KB generation skill that agents can use to draft a knowledge article with Now Assist.
        - [Configure skill for context menu](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-configuring-context-menu-skill.md) -- Configure the knowledge content recommendation skill so that the agents can use the Now Assist context menu to expand or shorten a knowledge article in TinyMCE. With the new content editor available in the Knowledge Center, agents can create and edit knowledge articles using custom instructions.
        - [Configure skills with custom prompts for knowledge article templates](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-assist-configure-custom-prompts-for-templates.md) -- Now Assist in Knowledge Management has extended the out-of-box (OOB) KB generation skill to create custom prompts for OOB and custom knowledge article templates.
          - [Guidelines for creating prompts](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/guidelines-for-creating-prompts.md) -- The guidelines help to write prompts for Out-of-Box (OOB) and custom knowledge article templates. Follow these instructions to create ideal prompts that optimize the use of AI models for generating knowledge articles.
        - [Configure skill to identify duplicate articles](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-configuring-identify-duplicate-article-skill.md) -- Configure the Knowledge content management skill to enable admins and managers to activate the Now Assist Identify duplicate articles skill.
        - [Configure Article Optimization skill and prompts](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/configure-kc-AO-skill.md) -- Create custom prompts for the default Article Optimization (AO) skill in the Knowledge Center application and prompts using standalone custom skills.
          - [Activate Article Optimization skill](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/activate-kc-AO-skill.md) -- Activate the Knowledge Center Article Optimization skill to enable use of article optimization features for generating knowledge articles in the Knowledge Center.
        - [Configure custom AI-based scans for Article Optimization](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/configure-custom-ai-based-AO-scans.md) -- The Out-of-Box (OOB) Knowledge Center settings provide default configurations for article optimization scans. However, you can customize scans for article optimization.
        - [Configure Now Assist Skills for potential gaps](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/configure-na-km.md) -- The Potential Gaps also known as the knowledge gaps feature identifies missing knowledge articles. This functionality is available only when the Knowledge Gaps skill is activated in Now Assist Admin. Configuring this skill is necessary when working with gaps.
        - [Configure Now Assist skills for merging articles](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/configure-merge-article.md) -- To merge the duplicate knowledge articles, activate Now Assist skills for merging the duplicate articles. Once configured, these skills enable the system to merge duplicate articles effectively, ensuring consistent and streamlined content management.
        - [Enable multilingual service in Now Assist](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-assist-enable-multilingual-service.md) -- Enable multilingual service in Now Assist Admin for Knowledge Management to support different languages in various third-party AI model providers. Select the languages that you want enabled for each of the AI model providers and enable native and dynamic translation support.
      - [Use Now Assist in Knowledge Management](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/using-now-assist-in-km.md) -- Now Assist in Knowledge Management enhances the agent experience by using artificial intelligence (AI) to generate a new knowledge article based on the resolution provided to a case or an incident.
        - [Generate a knowledge article from Now Assist panel](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-generate-article-NApanel.md) -- Generate a new knowledge article for a case or an incident from the Now Assist panel.
        - [Generate knowledge article in classic environment](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-generate-article-coreui.md) -- As an author or an agent, generate new Knowledge articles using Now Assist on tasks within the classic environment.
          - [Generate article in classic environment for authors](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-generate-article-coreui.md) -- As an author or an agent, generate new Knowledge articles using Now Assist on tasks within the classic environment.
        - [Generate article with multi-language](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-generate-article-using-multilanguage-support.md) -- Now Assist in Knowledge Management provides multi-language support for Knowledge article generation. Generate knowledge articles in multiple languages with the Now Assist multi-language support for Knowledge Management. As an agent, you can generate articles from incidents, cases and other task types that are documented in languages other than English by using a language selector configured by the admin.
        - [Edit article with context menu](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-generate-article-using-context-menu.md) -- Elaborate or shorten content within a Knowledge article using the Now Assist context menu feature that enables agents to leverage the generative AI capabilities of Now Assist in Knowledge Management.
          - [Supported tags in content recommendation](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/Now-Assist-generate-article-using-context-menu.md) -- Elaborate or shorten content within a Knowledge article using the Now Assist context menu feature that enables agents to leverage the generative AI capabilities of Now Assist in Knowledge Management.

        - [Merge duplicate articles](now-assist-in-knowledge-management/merge-duplicate-articles.md) -- Merge multiple duplicate knowledge articles into a single, consolidated article using Now Assist in Knowledge Management. This task helps knowledge managers reduce redundancy, preserve source article traceability, and verify readers access the most accurate and recent information.
    - [Response templates](response-templates-templated-snippets.md) -- Resolve cases or support issues faster and more efficiently with response templates. Response templates (formerly known as templated snippets) are reusable messages that can be copied to case or task forms to provide quick and consistent messages to users, or to display standard chat response messages to requesters in Agent Chat.
      - [Activate](activate-templated-snippets.md) -- You can activate response templates (formerly known as templated snippets) [com.sn_templated_snip] if you have the admin role. This plugin includes demo data and activates related plugins if they are not already active.
        - [Components installed with response templates](components-installed-with-response-templates.md) -- Several types of components are installed with activation of the Templated Responses (com.sn_templated_snip) plugin, including tables and user roles.
      - [Create a response template](create-modify-templated-snippet.md) -- Create or modify a response template (formerly known as a templated snippet) to define a reusable message that can be copied to case or task forms or used in Agent Chat sessions to provide quick and consistent messages to users.
  - [Manage service capabilities](manage-services.md) -- Give your customers tailored catalogs for products, and create portals for service requests, using the ServiceNow Service Catalog app.
    - [Self-service](self-service/self-service-landing.md) -- The Self-Service application gives supported end users a clean, simple front end to their IT support organization.

        - [Service catalog requests](self-service/p_ServiceCatalogRequests.md) -- This page contains links to content on service catalog requests.

          - [Place a request](self-service/t_PlaceARequest.md) -- Use ServiceNow's web-based interface for ordering predefined goods and services.

It also maintains a relationship between the requested item, and its associated incidents or change requests.
