      - [Connect Chat](connect/c_Collaboration.md) -- Connect Chat is a real-time messaging tool that enables users to chat with individuals and groups, quickly share files, and collaborate on any record by connecting with the right people instantly.
        - [Connect Chat use](connect/c_CollaborationUse.md) -- All users have access to Connect Chat, a convenient way to stay updated on all the people and documents you work with in a familiar chat interface.
          - [Start a direct or group conversation](connect/t_StartAConversation.md) -- You can start a conversation with one or more users in Connect Chat.
          - [Follow a record in Connect](connect/t_StartARecordConversation.md) -- You can follow a record in Connect Chat to track activity as it happens, in a familiar chat interface. When you follow a record, you become a member of a record conversation.
          - [Edit basic conversation details](connect/t_EditBasicConversationDetails.md) -- In Connect Chat, you can customize the avatar, name, and description for a group or record conversation.
          - [Add or remove conversation members](connect/t_AddOrRemoveConversationMembers.md) -- In Connect Chat, any member of a group or record conversation can add or remove other conversation members. In a direct conversation with only two people, you cannot add members to the conversation after you create it.
          - [Create a Connect conversation from a Visual Task Board](connect/t_ConversationVTB.md) -- Create a Connect Chat conversation from a Visual Task Board to collaborate with board members and keep track of activity as it happens.
        - [Connect Chat administration](connect/c_CollaborationAdministration.md) -- Administrators can configure various performance settings and features of Connect Chat.
          - [Configure the email notification interval](connect/c_CollaborationAdministration.md) -- Administrators can configure various performance settings and features of Connect Chat.
          - [Enable record conversations for a table](connect/c_CollaborationAdministration.md) -- Administrators can configure various performance settings and features of Connect Chat.
          - [Prevent users from following records in Connect for all tables](connect/c_CollaborationAdministration.md) -- Administrators can configure various performance settings and features of Connect Chat.
          - [Prevent users from following records in Connect for a specific table](connect/c_CollaborationAdministration.md) -- Administrators can configure various performance settings and features of Connect Chat.
          - [Disable Connect Chat](connect/c_CollaborationAdministration.md) -- Administrators can configure various performance settings and features of Connect Chat.
      - [Connect Support](connect/c_ConnectSupport.md) -- Connect Support is a real-time messaging tool that enables support agents to easily track their support cases, find solutions, and resolve problems quickly.
        - [Monitor incoming Connect Support conversations](connect/t_MonitorIncomingConnectSupport.md) -- In the support tab of the Connect sidebar, you can monitor the queues for which you are an agent and accept incoming conversations.
        - [Share knowledge in a Connect Support conversation](connect/t_ShareKnowledgeInConnectSupport.md) -- The support view of the Connect workspace has a built-in knowledge tool that makes it easy to search for knowledge articles and share them in a conversation.
        - [Transfer a Connect Support conversation to a different agent or queue](connect/t_TransferAConnectSupportConvo.md) -- You can transfer a Connect Support conversation to a different agent in the queue or to a different queue.
        - [Add a user to a Connect Support conversation](connect/add-users-to-support-chat.md) -- You can add additional users to a Connect Support conversation.
        - [Escalate a Connect Support conversation](connect/t_EscalateAConnectSupportConvo.md) -- If an escalation path is defined for a Connect Support conversation, you can use a shortcut to escalate a Connect Support conversation to a different queue.
        - [Connect Support chat states](connect/connect-support-chat-states.md) -- Connect Support chats move through specific states.
        - [Connect Support administration](connect/c_ConnectSupportAdministration.md) -- Administrators can configure various performance settings and features of Connect Support.
          - [Migrate from legacy chat to Connect Support](connect/t_ConnectSupportMigration.md) -- Connect Support is an extension of Connect. Users designated as support agents have access to the support tab of the connect sidebar. Do not use Connect Support concurrently with legacy chat.
          - [Properties for Connect Support](connect/r_PropertiesForConnectSupport.md) -- The Connect Support Properties page provides several configuration options specifically for Connect Support.
          - [Administer Connect Support queues](connect/t_AdministerConnectSupportQueues.md) -- You can define the support agents, default messages, schedule, and escalation path for each Connect Support queue.
          - [Make a Connect Support queue accessible to end users](connect/t_MakeConnectSupportQueueAccessible.md) -- To make a Connect Support queue accessible to end users, use the accepted URL format.
          - [Add a scripted extension point for Connect Support chats](connect/create-connect-extension-point.md) -- Implement the ConversationServerInteractionService extension point, which enables you to create custom scripts that use context variables from pre-chat surveys presented to requesters in the Agent Chat client.
          - [Connect Support metrics](connect/c_ConnectSupportMetrics.md) -- Optional plugins provide a homepage and Performance Analytics dashboards, which contain helpful Connect Support metrics.
            - [Use the Connect Support homepage](connect/c_ConnectSupportMetrics.md) -- Optional plugins provide a homepage and Performance Analytics dashboards, which contain helpful Connect Support metrics.
            - [Use the Connect Support dashboards](connect/c_ConnectSupportMetrics.md) -- Optional plugins provide a homepage and Performance Analytics dashboards, which contain helpful Connect Support metrics.
          - [Configure the add support users property](connect/config-add-support-user.md) -- To enable support agents to add users to a Connect Support conversation, add the glide.connect.support.add_members property.
        - [Service Desk Chat Platform Analytics Solutions](connect/service-desk-chat-content-pack.md) -- Platform Analytics Solutions contain preconfigured dashboards. These dashboards contain actionable data visualizations that help you improve your business processes and practices.
        - [Connect Support and Service Portal](connect/connect-support-sp.md) -- Use Connect Support in your portal to allow your users to ask questions or submit requests to support agents. You configure the instance options to control the appearance of your widget and how it functions.
          - [Configure Connect Support widget instance options](connect/connect-support-sp.md) -- Use Connect Support in your portal to allow your users to ask questions or submit requests to support agents. You configure the instance options to control the appearance of your widget and how it functions.
    - [Classic Orchestration](orchestration/r-orchestration.md) -- ServiceNow Orchestration extends the workflow engine to manage processes and to automate things outside of a ServiceNow instance.
      - [Introduction to Orchestration](orchestration/r-orchestration-introduction.md) -- Orchestration automates simple or complex multi-system tasks on remote services, servers, applications, and hardware.
      - [Domain separation and Orchestration](orchestration/domain-separation-orchestration.md) -- Domain separation is supported in Orchestration. Domain separation enables you to separate data, processes, and administrative tasks into logical groupings called domains. You can control several aspects of this separation, including which users can see and access data.
      - [Activate Orchestration](orchestration/t_ActivateOrchestration.md) -- The Orchestration (com.snc.runbook_automation) plugin is available within a separate subscription from the rest of the ServiceNow platform.
        - [List of Orchestration plugins](orchestration/list-orchestration-plugins.md) -- This table lists all plugins available for Orchestration and their dependencies.
        - [Orchestration Runtime plugin](orchestration/orchestration-runtime-plugin.md) -- The Orchestration – Runtime plugin enables other applications on the ServiceNow platform to automate tasks, without requiring an Orchestration subscription.
        - [List of Orchestration activities](orchestration/r_ListOfOrchestrationActivities.md) -- Use Orchestration activities in a Workflow to integrate with third-party systems.
        - [Available activity packs](orchestration/t_ActivateAnActivityPack.md) -- Activity packs are available with your subscription to Orchestration.
        - [Deprecated Orchestration activities](orchestration/r_OrchestrationBasicActivities.md) -- These Orchestration activities have been deprecated.
          - [Reactivate a deprecated activity](orchestration/t_ReactivateDeprecatedActivity.md) -- Orchestration activities that were converted to activity packs are deprecated for upgraded and new instances but can be reactivated for use in new workflows.
          - [SOAP Message workflow activity](orchestration/r_SOAPMessageActivity.md) -- The SOAP Message activity uses SOAP messages defined in the System Web Services plugin and can call the messages using a MID Server.
          - [Probes used by Orchestration](orchestration/r_OrchestrationProbes.md) -- Certain probes, controlled by MID Servers, perform Orchestration tasks on remote computers, such as restarting a server or creating virtual machines from templates.
          - [PowerShell activities](orchestration/c_OrchestrPowerShellActivities.md) -- PowerShell is built on the Windows .NET Framework and is designed to control and automate the administration of Windows machines and applications.
            - [Legacy Run PowerShell activity](orchestration/c_OrchestrPowerShellActivities.md) -- PowerShell is built on the Windows .NET Framework and is designed to control and automate the administration of Windows machines and applications.
            - [HResult codes](orchestration/c_OrchestrPowerShellActivities.md) -- PowerShell is built on the Windows .NET Framework and is designed to control and automate the administration of Windows machines and applications.
          - [SSH activities](orchestration/c_OrchestrationSSHActivities.md) -- SSH activities allow workflows to perform operations on Linux and Unix computers.
            - [Run Command activity](orchestration/c_OrchestrationSSHActivities.md) -- SSH activities allow workflows to perform operations on Linux and Unix computers.
            - [Run SCP activity](orchestration/c_OrchestrationSSHActivities.md) -- SSH activities allow workflows to perform operations on Linux and Unix computers.
      - [Orchestration applications](orchestration/c_OrchestrationApplications.md) -- Orchestration provides several applications with your subscription.
        - [Orchestration ROI](orchestration/c_OrchestrationROI.md) -- The Orchestration ROI application allows you to estimate and compute cost savings for tasks in your system that could be automated with Orchestration.
          - [Create Orchestration ROI labor rate cards](orchestration/t_CreateOrchROILaborRateCard.md) -- Before calculating your Orchestration ROI, you must create labor rate cards for the manual work that would be required to complete the tasks correlated to the ROI calculations.
          - [Create an Orchestration ROI automation entry record](orchestration/t_CreateOrchROIAutoEntryRecord.md) -- The ROI automation entry record couples the cost of performing a task with the specific workflow that automates the task, for the purpose of calculating the resulting savings.
          - [Create an Orchestration ROI correlated task rule](orchestration/t_CreateOrchROICorrTaskRule.md) -- The correlated task rule applies configurable conditions to fields in a selected table that tell the instance which manual tasks correspond to an automation entry record.
          - [Configure the time range for Orchestration ROI calculations](orchestration/t_ConfigTimeOrchROICalc.md) -- Select a date range to calculate the average number of tasks and the duration per month for the correlated tasks.
          - [Installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
            - [Tables installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
            - [Plugins installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
            - [Roles installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
            - [UI actions installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
            - [Script includes installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
            - [Client scripts installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
            - [Business rules installed with Orchestration ROI](orchestration/r_InstalledWithOrchestrationROI.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Orchestration - ROI plugin (com.snc.runbook_automation.roi).
          - [Orchestration ROI reports](orchestration/c_OrchestrationROIDashboards.md) -- Orchestration ROI provides a collection of standard and premium reports to help you calculate savings from automating services in your organization.
            - [Orchestration ROI standard reports](orchestration/r_OrchestrationROIStandardDashboard.md) -- The dashboard of standard ROI reports provides summaries, estimated costs, and calculated costs for automated processes. To access the ROI standard dashboard, navigate to Orchestration ROI Reports Dashboard .
              - [Schedule Orchestration ROI calculations for standard reports](orchestration/r_OrchestrationROIStandardDashboard.md) -- The dashboard of standard ROI reports provides summaries, estimated costs, and calculated costs for automated processes. To access the ROI standard dashboard, navigate to Orchestration ROI Reports Dashboard .
            - [Orchestration ROI premium reports](orchestration/r_OrchestrationROIPremiumDashboard.md) -- The premium dashboard provides detailed reports on calculated savings over time.
              - [Request Orchestration ROI premium reports](orchestration/r_OrchestrationROIPremiumDashboard.md) -- The premium dashboard provides detailed reports on calculated savings over time.
              - [Schedule Orchestration ROI calculations for premium reports](orchestration/r_OrchestrationROIPremiumDashboard.md) -- The premium dashboard provides detailed reports on calculated savings over time.
        - [Client Software Distribution](orchestration/c_ClientSoftwareDistribution.md) -- The Client Software Distribution (CSD) application allows administrators to distribute software from the service catalog using third-party management systems.
          - [Request client software distribution](orchestration/t_ActivateClientSWDistribution.md) -- Client software distribution requires the Orchestration - Client Software Distribution plugin (com.snc.orchestration.client_sf_distribution), which is available by request with a subscription to Orchestration.
          - [Installed with client software distribution](orchestration/r_IWClientSWDist.md) -- Several types of components are installed with client software distribution (CSD).
            - [Tables installed with client software distribution](orchestration/r_IWClientSWDist.md) -- Several types of components are installed with client software distribution (CSD).
            - [Plugins installed with client software distribution](orchestration/r_IWClientSWDist.md) -- Several types of components are installed with client software distribution (CSD).
            - [Roles installed with client software distribution](orchestration/r_IWClientSWDist.md) -- Several types of components are installed with client software distribution (CSD).
            - [Script includes installed with client software distribution](orchestration/r_IWClientSWDist.md) -- Several types of components are installed with client software distribution (CSD).
            - [Properties installed with client software distribution](orchestration/r_IWClientSWDist.md) -- Several types of components are installed with client software distribution (CSD).
            - [Workflows installed with client software distribution](orchestration/r_IWClientSWDist.md) -- Several types of components are installed with client software distribution (CSD).
          - [Create licenses and counters for distributed software](orchestration/t_CreateLicsAndCountsForDistSW.md) -- Licenses and software counters are associated with the software model and must be created if you want to track the license for software deployed by client software distribution (CSD).
            - [Client software distribution ordering process](orchestration/t_CreateLicsAndCountsForDistSW.md) -- Licenses and software counters are associated with the software model and must be created if you want to track the license for software deployed by client software distribution (CSD).
          - [Extend a software lease](orchestration/extend-software-lease.md) -- Users of software deployed by Client Software Distribution (CSD) can request the extension of a lease window, if the software is revocable by a software distribution system.
          - [Client software distribution from SCCM](orchestration/c_CSDFromAnSCCMHost.md) -- You can use Client Software Distribution (CSD) to deploy and revoke software deployments from Microsoft System Center Configuration Management (SCCM) and manage distributions on SCCM hosts.
            - [Configuring SCCM](orchestration/configuring-sccm.md) -- Follow the SCCM configuration procedures in the order shown.
              - [Configure the Application Administrator role on the SCCM server](orchestration/t-config-SCCM-deployment-manager.md) -- To deploy software using ServiceNow Client Software Distribution (CSD), ensure that an SCCM administrative user has the correct permissions to deploy software and that PowerShell is properly configured.
              - [Update the SCCM cmdlet libraries](orchestration/t-update-sccm-cmdlet-libraries.md) -- Ensure that the System Center Configuration Manager SCCM Cmdlet Library is up-to-date.
              - [Configure the MID Server for SCCM activities](orchestration/config-mid-sccm-activities.md) -- To use a MID Server with Microsoft System Center Configuration Management (SCCM) activities, configure it to communicate with the SCCM server.
              - [Create Windows credentials for SCCM deployments](orchestration/t_CreateWinCredSCCM.md) -- Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) requires the appropriate credentials to deploy applications using the Client Software Distribution application.
              - [Retrieve SCCM data for client software distribution](orchestration/t_RetrieveSCCMDataClientSWDist.md) -- Retrieve the collections and the list of applications available for deployment from your SCCM servers.
                - [SCCM server Discovery for client software distribution](orchestration/t_RetrieveSCCMDataClientSWDist.md) -- Retrieve the collections and the list of applications available for deployment from your SCCM servers.
              - [Set up a software model for an SCCM application](orchestration/t_CreateSWModelSCCMApp.md) -- Using the applications discovered on the SCCM server, set up a software model used by the Software Asset Management application to manage licenses.
              - [Define an SCCM configuration](orchestration/t_ConfigureSCCMCollections.md) -- To create catalog items for SCCM software deployment or to configure your instance to revoke software through SCCM, you must first associate that software with a collection through an SCCM configuration.
              - [Create a catalog item for an SCCM application](orchestration/t_CreateCatItemSCCMApp.md) -- Using the applications discovered on the SCCM server, create a catalog item for an application you want to offer for distribution from the service catalog.
            - [SCCM deployment](orchestration/r_SCCMDeploymentProcessWorkflow.md) -- Ordering an SCCM application from a client software distribution (CSD) catalog item in the service catalog triggers the Order Client Software workflow.
            - [Client software distribution validation process](orchestration/c_ClientSWDistValidProcess.md) -- After SCCM deploys software to a target computer, client software distribution (CSD) detects the installation and validates its status.
            - [SCCM software revocation](orchestration/c_SCCMSoftwareRevocation.md) -- An administrator can revoke software without any user interaction using Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) if the software configuration specifies an SCCM uninstall collection, even if the software was installed by some other process or user.
              - [Revoke software deployed through the service catalog](orchestration/c_SCCMSoftwareRevocation.md) -- An administrator can revoke software without any user interaction using Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) if the software configuration specifies an SCCM uninstall collection, even if the software was installed by some other process or user.
              - [Revoke unentitled software](orchestration/c_SCCMSoftwareRevocation.md) -- An administrator can revoke software without any user interaction using Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) if the software configuration specifies an SCCM uninstall collection, even if the software was installed by some other process or user.
              - [SCCM software revocation workflow](orchestration/c_SCCMSoftwareRevocation.md) -- An administrator can revoke software without any user interaction using Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) if the software configuration specifies an SCCM uninstall collection, even if the software was installed by some other process or user.
          - [Client software distribution dashboard](orchestration/c_ClientSWDistributionDash.md) -- The CSD dashboard provides a collection of visual reports for the Client Software Distribution application.
          - [Client software distribution extension framework](orchestration/c_CSDExtensionFramework.md) -- Client software distribution (CSD) provides built-in extension points for integrating a ServiceNow instance with client software distribution providers, such as Casper, Altiris, or LANDesk.
            - [Configure client software distribution providers](orchestration/t_ConfigCSDProviders.md) -- Identify the provider and specify the workflows and extension points for a customized software distribution process.
            - [Client software distribution extension keys](orchestration/r_CSDExtensionKeys.md) -- Client software distribution (CSD) extension keys allow you to customize the deployment and revocation of software from distribution providers.
            - [Client software distribution extension points](orchestration/r_CSDExtensionPoints.md) -- The client software distribution (CSD) extension points create the customization code for the specific provider and the extension keys.
      - [Orchestration examples](orchestration/c_OrchestrationExamples.md) -- These examples demonstrate how Orchestration can be used to automate common tasks.
        - [Active Directory automation example](orchestration/c_OrchExmplActiveDirUserMgmt.md) -- A set of six Orchestration Active Directory activities enables organizations to automate their on-boarding/off-boarding processes with auditable, self-documenting workflows that save time and eliminate mistakes.
          - [Update Active Directory with Orchestration](orchestration/c_OrchExmplActiveDirUserMgmt.md) -- A set of six Orchestration Active Directory activities enables organizations to automate their on-boarding/off-boarding processes with auditable, self-documenting workflows that save time and eliminate mistakes.
      - [Orchestration activity packs](orchestration/c_OrchestrationActivityPacks.md) -- The base Orchestration system includes packs of custom activities you can use to automate typical IT and business processes in your network.
        - [Use an activity pack](orchestration/t_UseAnActivityDesignerPack.md) -- The Packs tab of the Workflow Editor contains any activity packs downloaded from the ServiceNow Store and any activity packs that you create.
        - [Active Directory activity pack](orchestration/c_OrchActiveDirectoryActivities.md) -- The Active Directory (AD) activity pack enables an administrator to create, delete, and manage objects in Windows Active Directory, such as users, groups, and computers, using a ServiceNow Orchestration workflow.
          - [Add User to Group AD activity](orchestration/r_AddUserToGroupADActivity.md) -- The Add User to Group activity adds a user to a group in Windows Active Directory.
          - [Change AD User Password activity](orchestration/r_ChangeADUserPassword.md) -- The Change AD User Password activity changes the password for an Active Directory user account.
          - [Create AD Object activity](orchestration/r_CreateADObject.md) -- The Create AD Object activity creates an object in Windows Active Directory.
          - [Disable AD User Account activity](orchestration/r_DisableADUserAccount.md) -- The Disable AD User Account activity disables a Windows Active Directory user account, making it inactive.
          - [Enable AD User Account activity](orchestration/r_EnableADUserAccount.md) -- The Enable AD User Account activity enables a Windows Active Directory user account, making it active.
          - [Is AD Account Locked activity](orchestration/r_IsADAccountLocked.md) -- The Is AD Account Locked activity determines whether an Active Directory user account is locked.
          - [Query AD activity](orchestration/r_QueryAD.md) -- The Query AD activity retrieves entries from the Windows Active Directory based on an LDAP search filter and stores the results as a JSON string that can be used in the data bus.
          - [Remove AD Object activity](orchestration/r_RemoveADObject.md) -- The Remove AD Object activity deletes an object from Windows Active Directory.
          - [Remove User from Group AD activity](orchestration/r_RemoveUserFromGroupADActivity.md) -- The Remove User from Group activity removes a user from a group in Windows Active Directory.
          - [Reset AD User Password activity](orchestration/r_ResetADUserPassword.md) -- The Reset AD User Password activity resets the password of a user account in Windows Active Directory.
          - [Unlock AD Account activity](orchestration/r_UnlockADAccount.md) -- The Unlock AD Account activity unlocks a locked Active Directory user account.
          - [Update AD Object activity](orchestration/r_UpdateADObject.md) -- The Update AD Object activity updates an object in Windows Active Directory.
        - [Azure AD activity pack](orchestration/c_AzureADActivityPack.md) -- The Azure AD activity pack enables an administrator to automate employee onboarding and offboarding functions on Azure AD and manage Office 365 licensing.
          - [Add User to Group Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_AddUserToGroupAzureADActivity.md) -- The Add User to Group activity adds a user to an existing security group in Azure Active Directory.
          - [Assign User License Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_AssignUserLicenseAzureADActivity.md) -- The Assign User License activity assigns an additional Office 365 software license to the named user.
          - [Create User Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_CreateUserAzureADActivity.md) -- The Create User activity creates a user for the Azure Active Directory tenant.
          - [Delete User Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_DeleteUserAzureADActivity.md) -- The Delete User activity deletes the named user's account from the Azure Active Directory tenant.
          - [Get User Info Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_GetUserInfoAzureADActivity.md) -- The Get User Info activity returns the named user's information from the Azure Active Directory.
          - [Remove User from Group Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_RemoveUserFromGroupAzureActivity.md) -- The Remove User from Group activity removes an existing user from a security group in Azure Active Directory.
          - [Remove User License Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_RemoveUserLicenseAzureADActivity.md) -- The Remove User License activity revokes the specified Office 365 software license for the named user.
          - [Reset User Password Azure AD activity](orchestration/r_ResetUserPasswordAzureADActivity.md) -- The Reset User Password activity changes the named user's Azure Active Directory password.
        - [Exchange activity pack](orchestration/c_OrchestrationExchangeActivities.md) -- The Exchange activity pack allows workflows to manage Microsoft Exchange mail systems.
          - [Configure MID Server for Exchange](orchestration/t_ConfigureMIDServerForExchange.md) -- Configure a MID Server with defined IP ranges to use Exchange.
          - [Pass multivalued properties with an Exchange activity](orchestration/t_PassMultivaluedProperties.md) -- To set a multivalued property using an Exchange activity, you must use a specific parameter format.
          - [Create Address List activity](orchestration/r_CreateAddressListActivity.md) -- The Create Address List activity creates a new address list that acts as an alias for emailing all users in the group.
          - [Delete Address List activity](orchestration/r_DeleteAddressListActivity.md) -- The Delete Address List activity removes an existing address list from an Exchange server.
          - [Get Address List activity](orchestration/r_GetAddressListActivity.md) -- The Get AddressList activity retrieves all attributes from the specified Exchange address list.
          - [Move Address List activity](orchestration/r_MoveAddressListActivity.md) -- The Move Address List activity moves a Microsoft Exchange address list to another location in the address hierarchy.
          - [Set Address List activity](orchestration/r_SetAddressListActivity.md) -- The Set Address List activity modifies a Microsoft Exchange address list.
          - [Update Address List activity](orchestration/r_UpdateAddressListActivity.md) -- The Update Address List activity adds or removes users from the specified address list and immediately applies any cached address list changes to the server.
          - [Create Mailbox activity](orchestration/r_CreateMailboxActivity.md) -- The Create Mailbox activity creates a new Active Directory user, if that user does not already exist, and a Microsoft Exchange mailbox for that user.
          - [Delete Mailbox activity](orchestration/r_DeleteMailboxActivity.md) -- The Delete Mailbox activity deletes the Microsoft Exchange mailbox of an Active Directory user.
          - [Disable Mailbox activity](orchestration/r_DisableMailboxActivity.md) -- The Disable Mailbox activity disables the mailbox associated with a specified Active Directory account.
          - [Enable Mailbox activity](orchestration/r_EnableMailboxActivity.md) -- The Enable Mailbox activity creates a new mailbox for an existing Active Directory user.
          - [Get Mailbox activity](orchestration/r_GetMailboxActivity.md) -- The Get Mailbox activity gets the mailbox for the specified user or all mailboxes on a Microsoft Exchange server.
          - [Set Mailbox activity](orchestration/r_SetMailboxActivity.md) -- The Set Mailbox activity modifies the settings of an existing Microsoft Exchange mailbox.
        - [F5 Network Management activity pack](orchestration/c_OrchF5LoadBalancerActivities.md) -- Orchestration provides custom activities for configuring elements of an F5 load balancer, including pools, pool members, and the virtual servers contained in the pool.
          - [Add F5 Pool Member activity](orchestration/r_AddF5PoolMemberActivity.md) -- The Add F5 Pool Member activity adds a member to an F5 load balancer pool.
          - [Get F5 Pool Member activity](orchestration/r_GetF5PoolMemberActivity.md) -- The Get F5 Pool Member activity returns the pool members and verifies the pool's status.
          - [Delete F5 Virtual Server activity](orchestration/r_DeleteF5VirtualServerActivity.md) -- The Delete F5 Virtual Server activity deletes a virtual server from an F5 load balancer pool.
          - [Check F5 Pool activity](orchestration/r_CheckF5PoolActivity.md) -- The Check F5 Pool activity checks the status of a pool to determine if it is available to accept traffic.
          - [Add F5 Profile to Virtual Server activity](orchestration/r_AddF5ProfileToVirtualServerActivity.md) -- The Add F5 Profile to Virtual Server activity adds a profile to a virtual server assigned to an F5 load balancer pool.
          - [Modify F5 Virtual Server activity](orchestration/r_ModifyF5VirtualServerActivity.md) -- The Modify F5 Virtual Server activity configures a virtual server assigned to an F5 load balancer pool with irules and a vlan.
          - [Add F5 Virtual Server activity](orchestration/r_AddF5VirtualServerActivity.md) -- The Add F5 Virtual Server activity adds a virtual server.
          - [Delete F5 Pool Member activity](orchestration/r_DeleteF5PoolMemberActivity.md) -- The Delete F5 Pool Member activity deletes an F5 load balancer pool member.
          - [Delete F5 Pool activity](orchestration/r_DeleteF5PoolActivity.md) -- The Delete F5 Pool activity deletes an F5 load balancer pool.
          - [Add F5 Pool activity](orchestration/r_AddF5PoolActivity.md) -- The Add F5 Pool activity creates the F5 load balancer pool.
        - [Infoblox DDI activity pack](orchestration/c_InfobloxDDIActivityPack.md) -- This activity pack supports Infoblox DDI management through ServiceNow Orchestration.
          - [Create a REST credential for Infoblox activities](orchestration/t_CreateRESTCredRecInfoBloxAct.md) -- All Infoblox activities require a REST credential to manage IP addresses from the Infoblox DDI server.
          - [Infoblox workflows](orchestration/r_InfobloxWorkflows.md) -- The Infobox DDI activity pack includes two default workflows, one that reserves and registers IP addresses and another that releases IP addresses.
          - [Infoblox roles and permissions](orchestration/infoblox-roles-permissions.md) -- Define the roles and permissions that the Infoblox activities need to access the various resources on the Infoblox server.
          - [Infoblox DHCP activities](orchestration/c_InfobloxDHCPActivities.md) -- The Infoblox DHCP activities manage the IP addresses reserved for DHCP in your network.

          - [Add to User Collection activity](orchestration/r_AddToUserCollectionActivity.md) -- The Add to User Collection activity adds a user to a Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) user collection.
          - [Get Applications activity](orchestration/r_GetApplicationsActivity.md) -- The Get Applications activity returns a list of all the applications available on a Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) server.
          - [Get Deployments activity](orchestration/r_GetDeploymentsActivity.md) -- The Get Deployments activity returns the list of deployments performed by Orchestration using a Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) server.
          - [Get Device Collections activity](orchestration/r_GetDeviceCollectionsActivity.md) -- The Get Device Collections activity returns the list of available device collections on a Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) host.
          - [Get User Collections activity](orchestration/r_GetUserCollectionsActivity.md) -- The Get User Collections activity returns the list of available user collections on a Microsoft System Center Configuration Manager (SCCM) server.

        - [SFTP File Transfer activity pack](orchestration/c_OrchestrationSFTPActivityPack.md) -- Use the SFTP File Transfer activities to manage files and directories on an SFTP server and to move files from one SFTP server to another.

          - [Create Directory activity](orchestration/r_CreateDirectoryActivity.md) -- The Create Directory activity creates a new directory on an SFTP server.
          - [Get File List activity](orchestration/r_GetFileListActivity.md) -- The Get File List activity returns a list of files from a given directory and its subdirectories on an SFTP server (source host).
          - [Remove File or Directory activity](orchestration/r_RemoveFileOrDirectoryActivity.md) -- The Remove File or Directory activity removes a file or a directory on an SFTP server, including subdirectories, when configured.
          - [Rename File or Directory activity](orchestration/r_RenameFileOrDirectoryActivity.md) -- The Rename File or Directory activity renames a file or directory to a new name on an SFTP server.

    - [Task Communications Management](task-communications-management/tcm-landing-page.md) -- Task Communications Management (TCM) provides a framework for defining and managing a communication process for any entity that extends the task table.
      - [Task Communications Management process](task-communications-management/task-communications-management.md) -- Task Communications Management helps you to streamline the process by creating designated communication plans for effective communication during an event. The communication plan, with its defined tasks, helps you to focus on resolving the current issue instead of deciding when to send a communication, what information to share, and whom to inform.
      - [Task Communications Management plugins](task-communications-management/tcm-plugins.md) -- The Task Communications Management plugins is activated in the base system. The plugin includes demo data.
        - [Activate Task Communications Management](task-communications-management/activate-tcm-plugin.md) -- The Task Communications Management plugin (com.snc.task_communication_management) is activated when you activate the Incident Communications Management plugin (com.snc.iam) or the Incident Management - Major Incident Management plugin (com.snc.incident.mim). This plugin includes demo data and activates related plugins if they are not already active.
          - [Components installed with Task Communications Management](task-communications-management/components-installed-with-tcm.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Task Communications Management plugin, including tables and user roles.
        - [Install Collaboration Services for Task Communications Management](task-communications-management/install-collab-services-for-tcm.md) -- Install the Collaboration Services for Task Communications Management plugin (sn_tcm_collab_hook) from ServiceNow Store to get the Slack functionality.
          - [Components installed with Collaboration Services for Task Communications Management](task-communications-management/components-with-collab-services-tcm.md) -- Several types of components are installed with the Collaboration Services for Task Communications Management (sn_tcm_collab_hook) plugin, including tables.
      - [Working with Task Communications Management](task-communications-management/working-with-tcm.md) -- The section covers topics that help you with a variety of technical concepts related to Task Communications Management such as creating communication plan definition, task definition, channel definition, and contact definition.
        - [Define a communication plan](task-communications-management/create-comm-plan-definition.md) -- Define a communication plan for a task record to specify communication task and contact definitions. When specified conditions for the plan definition are met, the communication plan and its associated records are automatically attached to the task record, eliminating manual effort.
          - [Define a communication task](task-communications-management/create-comm-task-definition.md) -- Define a communication task for a communication plan. When a plan gets attached to a table, the tasks related to the plan need to be executed to resolve the issue. You can associate multiple tasks with a communication plan.
          - [Define a communication channel](task-communications-management/create-comm-channel-definition.md) -- Define a communication channel for each communication task to determine which mode of communication to use for the task when a plan is attached to a table.
          - [Define a communication contact](task-communications-management/create-comm-contact-definition.md) -- Define the recipients of a particular plan to determine the target audience involved in each communication task and the responsibilities the recipients are expected to handle. A notification for a task is sent to all individuals specified for that task.
        - [Define a recipient list for communication contact](task-communications-management/define-recipient-list-comm-contact.md) -- A recipient list lets you specify a filter to fetch a list of users. Use the recipient list as the contact for a particular communication plan.
        - [Create a plan type](task-communications-management/define-plan-type.md) -- Create the communication plan type and specify whether the plan type is applicable globally or to a particular table. When you create the communication plan type, it is referenced in the communication plan definition. The type of the communication plan helps you to filter the plan by its type, generate a report of a particular plan type, or create SLAs for any communication plan.
        - [Create a task type](task-communications-management/define-task-type.md) -- Create the communication task type and specify whether the task is applicable to a particular table or is globally applicable. The communication task type helps you to filter a task by its type, generate a report of a particular task type, or create SLAs for any communication task.
        - [Define contact responsibilities](task-communications-management/define-contact-responsibilities.md) -- Define the different contacts or target audience involved in the communication process and their responsibility to understand the expectations from those contacts throughout the process.
        - [View communication channel configuration](task-communications-management/view-comm-channel-config.md) -- View the configuration of different communication channels that are used to contact the target audience in the communication process. By default, the communication channels available are email, SMS, Slack, and conference.
        - [Configure Slack for a communication task](task-communications-management/configure-slack-for-a-comm-task.md) -- Configure Slack as a mode of communication for a communication task to send direct message to the users.
    - [Classic Workflow](legacy-workflow/c_WorkflowOverview.md) -- Workflow is a legacy application that provided a drag-and-drop interface for automating multi-step processes across the platform. Each workflow consists of a sequence of activities, such as generating records, notifying users of pending approvals, or running scripts. The graphical Workflow Editor represents workflows visually as a type of flowchart. It shows activities as boxes labeled with information about that activity and transitions from one activity to the next as lines connecting the boxes.
      - [Getting started with workflows](legacy-workflow/getting-started-workflows.md) -- The graphical Workflow Editor provides a drag-and-drop interface for automating multi-step processes across the platform.

        - [Workflow validation](legacy-workflow/c_WorkflowValidation.md) -- Workflow validation examines different characteristics of a workflow to locate issues that might prevent the workflow from being published or cause it to fail. The validation report summarizes the results of each separate workflow validation.
          - [Workflow validator](legacy-workflow/r_WorkflowValidator.md) -- ServiceNow offers several workflow validators for workflow designers to test their workflows.
            - [ValidateTransitionOut](legacy-workflow/r_VldtTransitionOut.md) -- The ValidateTransitionOut validator finds activity conditions with no exit transitions.
            - [ValidateTransitionIn](legacy-workflow/c_ValidateTransitionIn.md) -- The ValidateTransitionIn validator finds activities that do not have inbound transitions and cannot execute in the workflow.
            - [ValidateDanglingTransition](legacy-workflow/r_VldtDanglingTransition.md) -- The ValidateDanglingTransition validator finds and reports any transitions that do not terminate on an activity.
            - [ValidateSubflows](legacy-workflow/r_ValidateSubflows.md) -- The ValidateSubflows validator detects any workflows included as subflows that are either inactive, deleted, or not available as a published workflow for the current user.
            - [ValidateScriptForCurrentDotUpdate](legacy-workflow/c_VldtScptCurrentDotUpdate.md) -- The ValidateScriptForCurrentDotUpdate validator finds workflow activities with scripts that use the current.update() function.
            - [ValidateLowestCommonTable](legacy-workflow/r_VldtLowestCommonTable.md) -- The ValidateLowestCommonTable validator reports the lowest table in the Glide hierarchy that the workflow uses.
            - [ValidateTableChange](legacy-workflow/r_VldtTableChange.md) -- The ValidateTableChange validator reports any activities in the workflow that are invalid given the table associated with the workflow version.
            - [ValidateParentFlow](legacy-workflow/c_ValidateParentFlow.md) -- The ValidateParentFlow validator reports any workflows that use the workflow as a subflow.
            - [ValidateSingleEnd](legacy-workflow/r_ValidateSingleEnd.md) -- The ValidateSingleEnd validator finds and identifies multiple End activities in a single workflow.
            - [ValidateUpdateSetDependencies](legacy-workflow/r_VldtUpdtStDpnd.md) -- The ValidateUpdateSetDependencies validator identifies all the subflows called in the current workflow and determines if any of those subflows are being edited in a different (in progress) update set.
            - [ValidateUpdateSetParentDependencies](legacy-workflow/r_VldtUpdtStPtDpnd.md) -- The ValidateUpdateSetParentDependencies validator identifies all the workflows that call the current workflow as a subflow and determines if any of those parent workflows are being edited in a different update set that is in progress.
            - [ValidateInputVarUpdateSetDependencies](legacy-workflow/r_VldtInptVrUpdtStDpd.md) -- The ValidateInputVarUpdateSetDependencies validator examines update sets to ensure that workflow input variables for a given workflow have not been deleted in different update sets than those currently In progress.
            - [ValidateWorkflowEndStages](legacy-workflow/r_VldtWorkflowEndStages.md) -- The ValidateWorkflowEndStages validator checks that in workflows with stages, the end activity of the workflow has a stage named Complete or Completed.
            - [ValidateWorkflowStageColumn](legacy-workflow/r_VldtWkflwStageColumn.md) -- The ValidateWorkflowStageColumn validator detects and reports when the stage field (stage column) for a workflow is not correct or is unusable.
            - [ValidateWorkflowStateValues](legacy-workflow/r_VldtWorkflowStateValues.md) -- The ValidateWorkflowStateValues validator checks a number of stage aspects in workflow activities for correctness.

        - [Workflow gauges](legacy-workflow/t_UseTheWorkflowOpsDashboard.md) -- On the Workflow Operations Dashboard, view and add widgets to help you monitor workflows. Review the performance of workflows and determine which workflows must be improved.
      - [Workflow performance timing](legacy-workflow/c_WorkflowPerformanceTiming.md) -- The workflow engine can generate detailed performance timing data that is useful for troubleshooting slow workflows.
        - [Workflow estimated run time properties](legacy-workflow/c_WorkflowPerformanceTiming.md) -- The workflow engine can generate detailed performance timing data that is useful for troubleshooting slow workflows.

      - [Workflow activities](workflow-activities/c_WorkflowActivities.md) -- Workflow activity properties reference
        - [Workflow activities reference](workflow-activities/wf-activity-overview.md) -- Workflow activity reference, organized by category.
          - [Approval and rollback workflow activities](workflow-activities/c_ApprovalAndRollbackActivities.md) -- Approval and rollback activities generate and manage approvals.
            - [Approval Action workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_ApprovalAction.md) -- The Approval Action activity performs an approval action on the current task.
            - [Approval Coordinator workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_ApprovalCoordinator.md) -- The Approval Coordinator activity creates an approval whose outcome depends on the outcome of one or more child activities, including one or more Approval - User, Approval - Group, and/or Manual Approval activities.
            - [Approval - Group workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_ApprovalGroup.md) -- The Approval - Group activity creates approval records for each member of a specified group.
            - [Approval - User workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_ApprovalUser.md) -- The Approval - User activity creates one or more individual user approvals.
            - [Generate workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_Generate.md) -- The Generate activity immediately creates task or approval records from any task or approval activities placed after the Generate activity in the workflow path. These pre-generated tasks and approvals start when the task and approval activities are reached during flow execution. This allows a task to have a set of associated pre-generated sequential tasks or approvals, but still require them to be completed in order.
            - [Manual Approvals workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_ManualApprovals.md) -- The Manual Approvals activity watches and manages any approvals that users add manually outside of the workflow process. This activity only selects approvals that are in the Not requested state.
            - [Rollback To workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_RollbackTo.md) -- The Rollback To activity transitions directly to the activity specified by the outgoing transition line arrow.
              - [Rollback To behavior](workflow-activities/r_RollbackTo.md) -- The Rollback To activity transitions directly to the activity specified by the outgoing transition line arrow.
              - [Rollback To activity](workflow-activities/r_RollbackTo.md) -- The Rollback To activity transitions directly to the activity specified by the outgoing transition line arrow.
          - [Condition Workflow activities](workflow-activities/r_ConditionActivites.md) -- Condition activities provide conditional branching and logical operation functionality for workflows.
            - [If workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_If.md) -- The If activity checks a condition or script to determine if a Yes or No transition should be taken.
            - [Switch workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_Switch.md) -- The Switch activity checks if the value of a passed field or variable is equivalent to one of several case values.
            - [Wait for condition workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_WaitForCondition.md) -- The Wait for condition activity causes the workflow to wait at this activity until the current record matches the specified condition.
            - [Wait for WF Event workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_WaitForWFEvent.md) -- The Wait for WF Event activity causes the workflow to wait at this activity until the specified event is fired.
          - [Workflow notification activities](workflow-activities/c_NotificationActivities.md) -- Notification workflow activities notify users of events that occur during the workflow.
            - [Create Event workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_CreateEvent.md) -- The Create Event activity adds an event to the event queue, but does not immediately fire the event.
            - [Notification workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_NotificationActivity.md) -- The Notification activity sends an email or SMS message to specified users or groups.
          - [Timer workflow activities](workflow-activities/c_TimerActivities.md) -- Timer activities pause workflows for set periods of time.
            - [SLA Percentage Timer workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_SLAPercentageTimer.md) -- The SLA Percentage Timer activity pauses the workflow for a duration equal to a percentage of an SLA.
            - [Timer workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_Timer.md) -- The Timer activity pauses the workflow for a specified period of time.
          - [Task workflow activities](workflow-activities/r_TaskActivities.md) -- Task activities create and modify workflow tasks.
            - [Add Worknote workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_AddWorknote.md) -- The Add Worknote activity adds text to the Worknotes field of the current incident record.
            - [Attachment Note workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_AttachmentNote.md) -- The Attachment Note activity adds an attachment to the current record.
            - [Catalog Task workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_CatalogTask.md) -- The Catalog Task activity creates a service catalog task record.
            - [Create Task workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_CreateTask.md) -- The Create Task activity generates a record on any of the tables that extend Task [task].
          - [Utility workflow activities](workflow-activities/c_UtilityActivities.md) -- Utility activities provide controls over the path of the workflow, and other useful tools.
            - [Branch workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_BranchActivity.md) -- The Branch activity splits the workflow into multiple transition paths from a single activity.
            - [Join workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_JoinActivity.md) -- The Join activity unites multiple execution paths into one transition.
            - [Lock workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_LockActivity.md) -- The Lock activity prevents other instances of this workflow from continuing past this activity until the lock is released.
            - [Log Message workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_LogMessageActivity.md) -- The Log Message activity writes a message to the workflow log.
            - [Log Trace Message workflow activity](workflow-activities/c_LogTraceMessage.md) -- The Log Trace Message activity writes a trace message to the workflow log.
            - [REST Message legacy workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_RESTMessageActivity.md) -- The legacy REST Message activity enables an administrator to override the REST endpoint or supply the variables configured in the REST Message module.
            - [Return Value workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_ReturnValueActivity.md) -- The Return Value activity returns a value to a parent workflow, when run from a subflow.
            - [Run Script workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_RunScriptActivity.md) -- The Run Script activity runs the specified script in the scope of the workflow version.
            - [Set Values workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_SetValuesActivity.md) -- The Set Values activity sets values on the current record when the workflow quiesces or ends.
            - [SOAP Message legacy workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_SOAPMessageActivity_1.md) -- The legacy SOAP Message activity uses SOAP messages defined in the System Web Services plugin and can call the messages using a MID Server.
            - [Turnstile workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_TurnstileActivity.md) -- The Turnstile activity limits how many times a workflow can pass through the same point.
            - [Unlock workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_UnlockActivity.md) -- The Unlock activity releases a lock that was previously placed by the Lock activity.
          - [Subflow activities in workflow](workflow-activities/c_SubflowActivities.md) -- Subflow activities run and manage workflows from a parent workflow.
            - [Parallel Flow Launcher workflow activity](workflow-activities/r_ParallelFlowLauncher.md) -- The Parallel Flow Launcher activity launches multiple subflows in parallel.
              - [WorkflowCoordinator object](workflow-activities/c_WorkflowCoordinatorObject.md) -- A WorkflowCoordinator object specifies which subflows to run and the input variables to pass to those subflows.
              - [Completed subflow values in scripts](workflow-activities/c_CompletedSubflowValuesInScripts.md) -- The Parallel Flow Launcher activity exposes additional variables you can use in scripts.
              - [Parallel Flow Launcher example](workflow-activities/r_ParallelFlowLauncherExample.md) -- This example shows how to use the Parallel Flow Launcheractivity with an array of input values and with a WorkflowCoordinator object.
        - [View workflow activity descriptions](workflow-activities/t_ViewActivityDescriptions.md) -- Tooltips are available for workflow activities to help you understand how to use each activity.
          - [Elements in workflow activity definitions](workflow-activities/t_ViewActivityDescriptions.md) -- Tooltips are available for workflow activities to help you understand how to use each activity.
        - [Add an activity to a workflow](workflow-activities/t_AddAnActivityToAWorkflow.md) -- Available activities are displayed in the Core, Packs, and Custom tabs in the Workflow Editor palette.
          - [Duplicate a workflow activity](workflow-activities/t_AddAnActivityToAWorkflow.md) -- Available activities are displayed in the Core, Packs, and Custom tabs in the Workflow Editor palette.
        - [Manage transitions between workflow activities](workflow-activities/t_MngTstnsWkflAct.md) -- Transitions define the processing path of the workflow, depending on conditions defined in each activity.
          - [Custom activity transitions](workflow-activities/t_MngTstnsWkflAct.md) -- Transitions define the processing path of the workflow, depending on conditions defined in each activity.
        - [Manage workflow activity conditions](workflow-activities/t_ManageActivityConditions.md) -- Activities contain default conditions that determine which transitions are followed.
          - [Activity result value](workflow-activities/t_ManageActivityConditions.md) -- Activities contain default conditions that determine which transitions are followed.
        - [Edit the workflow activity properties form](workflow-activities/t_EditActivitiesPropForm.md) -- Customize which workflow variables appear on an activity properties form and how the variables are arranged on the form.
        - [Using workflow approval activities and rolling back workflows](workflow-activities/wf-approvals-n-rollbacks.md) -- When you work with approvals, you need to understand how approval activities interact with approval engines, how to correct a skipped approval workflow activity, and how rollbacks work.
          - [Approval workflow activities and approval engines](workflow-activities/wf-approvals-n-rollbacks.md) -- When you work with approvals, you need to understand how approval activities interact with approval engines, how to correct a skipped approval workflow activity, and how rollbacks work.
          - [Correct a skipped workflow approval activity](workflow-activities/wf-approvals-n-rollbacks.md) -- When you work with approvals, you need to understand how approval activities interact with approval engines, how to correct a skipped approval workflow activity, and how rollbacks work.
        - [Using variables in Notify workflow activities](workflow-activities/c_UsingVariablesInNotifyActivities.md) -- Certain Notify workflow activities support variable substitution for reading text to callers.
        - [Use multiple timer activities in one workflow](workflow-activities/c_MltTmActWkflw.md) -- Workflow timer activities store data independently of each other in an activity-specific scratchpad.
        - [Publish a custom workflow activity](workflow-activities/c_PublishCustomActivities.md) -- When a user creates a custom activity and saves or submits it, that activity appears in the Custom and Packs tabs of the designer palette, but is only visible to the user who created it.
        - [Workflow activity pinning](workflow-activities/c_ActivityPinning.md) -- Workflow administrators can pin a custom activity to prevent the system from automatically updating that activity when a new version is downloaded from the ServiceNow Store.
  - [RaptorDB](raptordb.md) -- RaptorDB, the new ServiceNow database, optimizes ServiceNow AI Platform workloads, enabling your organization to support more users, workflows, and data on a single platform.

  - [HTTP Response Headers](http-response-header.md) -- A response header is a simple name-value pair used in an HTTP response to provide additional information about page content or how the client should process it.
    - [Configure](configure-http-response-header.md) -- Configure standard name-value pairs for HTTP response headers. You designate if the configuration applies to all pages, or to specific types (Service Portal, UI Page, or UX application record).
